\"reported_measurement_stated\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> dict[str, int]:\n    specs = [\n        (\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_measurement_stated\", values\"),\n    ]\n    counts: dict[str, int] = {}\n    for relation, universe, basis in specs:\n        result = tv.run_derivation(\n            relation,\n            completeness=Completeness(\n
